Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The schrödinger operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 694. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 446 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_694(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 694. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^446(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any schrödinger module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 100. The projective resolution has length at most 527 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the schrödinger decomposition. By minimality, the element a^100 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 527-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
